"""The `mc` entry point: every command group, the global options, and a quiet exit when the
reader of a pipe goes away."""

import os
import sys
from importlib.metadata import PackageNotFoundError, version
from typing import Annotated

import typer

from mission_control.cli.commands import account, admin, assignments, crew, me, missions
from mission_control.cli.commands.common import group
from mission_control.cli.env import Env

app = group(name="mc")
account.register(app)
app.add_typer(me.app, name="me")
app.add_typer(assignments.app, name="assignments")
app.add_typer(missions.app, name="missions")
app.add_typer(crew.app, name="crew")
app.add_typer(admin.users_app, name="users")
app.add_typer(admin.skills_app, name="skills")
app.add_typer(admin.org_app, name="org")


def _show_version(value: bool) -> None:
    if value:
        try:
            number = version("mission-control")
        except PackageNotFoundError:
            number = "unknown"
        typer.echo(f"mc {number}")
        raise typer.Exit()


@app.callback()
def root(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    show_version: Annotated[
        bool,
        typer.Option(
            "--version", help="Show the version and exit", callback=_show_version, is_eager=True
        ),
    ] = False,
) -> None:
    """Mission Control: plan missions, crew them and get them approved.

    Start with mc login, then mc inbox. Every command has --help.

    Exit codes: 0 OK, 1 refused, 2 usage, 3 not logged in, 4 server unreachable or busy,
    130 interrupted.
    """
    if ctx.obj is None:
        ctx.obj = Env()


def main() -> None:
    try:
        app(prog_name="mc")
    finally:
        _flush_quietly()


def _flush_quietly() -> None:
    """`mc ... | head` closes the pipe early; don't end with a broken-pipe traceback."""
    try:
        sys.stdout.flush()
    except BrokenPipeError:
        devnull = os.open(os.devnull, os.O_WRONLY)
        os.dup2(devnull, sys.stdout.fileno())
"""Where the CLI finds the server, and where it keeps the session from `mc login`: a small JSON
file that only the user can read."""

import json
import os
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path

DEFAULT_API_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8000"
_CREDENTIALS_FILE = "credentials.json"


def default_config_dir() -> Path:
    return Path.home() / ".config" / "mission-control"


def api_url() -> str:
    """The server's base URL. `MC_API_URL` overrides the local default."""
    return (os.environ.get("MC_API_URL") or "").strip().rstrip("/") or DEFAULT_API_URL


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Credentials:
    token: str
    email: str
    expires_at: str | None = None


class CredentialStore:
    """The session token on disk. The file is written with mode 0600 inside a 0700 directory,
    and replaced atomically so a crash never leaves half a file."""

    def __init__(self, directory: Path) -> None:
        self.directory = directory

    @property
    def path(self) -> Path:
        return self.directory / _CREDENTIALS_FILE

    def load(self) -> Credentials | None:
        """The saved session, or None when there isn't a usable one."""
        try:
            data = json.loads(self.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            return None
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            return None
        token = data.get("token")
        if not isinstance(token, str) or not token:
            return None
        expires_at = data.get("expires_at")
        return Credentials(
            token=token,
            email=str(data.get("email") or ""),
            expires_at=expires_at if isinstance(expires_at, str) else None,
        )

    def save(self, credentials: Credentials) -> None:
        self.directory.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        os.chmod(self.directory, 0o700)
        temporary = self.path.with_name(self.path.name + ".tmp")
        fd = os.open(temporary, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)
        try:
            os.fchmod(fd, 0o600)  # the mode given to open() is ignored if the file existed
            with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as file:
                json.dump(asdict(credentials), file)
        except BaseException:
            temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            raise
        os.replace(temporary, self.path)

    def clear(self) -> None:
        self.path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
"""What every command runs with: the HTTP client, the saved session, the terminal and whether
there's anyone at the keyboard. Tests build one with a mock client and a temporary config
directory; a real run builds the defaults."""

import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import httpx2

from mission_control.cli.client import TIMEOUT, Api
from mission_control.cli.config import CredentialStore, api_url, default_config_dir
from mission_control.cli.errors import NotLoggedIn
from mission_control.cli.prompts import Prompter, TerminalPrompter
from mission_control.cli.render import Terminal


@dataclass
class Env:
    http: httpx2.Client | None = None
    config_dir: Path | None = None
    interactive: bool | None = None  # None: ask whether stdin is a terminal
    color: bool | None = None  # None: decide from the terminal and NO_COLOR
    prompter: Prompter = field(default_factory=TerminalPrompter)
    json_output: bool = False  # set by a command run with --json
    _terminal: Terminal | None = field(default=None, init=False, repr=False)

    @property
    def store(self) -> CredentialStore:
        return CredentialStore(self.config_dir or default_config_dir())

    @property
    def term(self) -> Terminal:
        # Built on first use, so it writes to the streams in place when the command runs.
        if self._terminal is None:
            self._terminal = Terminal.for_streams(sys.stdout, sys.stderr, self.color)
        return self._terminal

    @property
    def is_interactive(self) -> bool:
        if self.interactive is not None:
            return self.interactive
        try:
            return sys.stdin.isatty()
        except (AttributeError, ValueError):
            return False

    def client(self) -> httpx2.Client:
        if self.http is None:
            self.http = httpx2.Client(base_url=api_url(), timeout=TIMEOUT)
        return self.http

    def anonymous_api(self) -> Api:
        return Api(self.client())

    def api(self) -> Api:
        """An API client for the logged-in user. A 401 clears the saved session."""
        credentials = self.store.load()
        if credentials is None:
            raise NotLoggedIn()
        return Api(self.client(), credentials.token, on_session_ended=self.store.clear)
"""The CLI's only way into Mission Control: JSON requests to the HTTP API through an `httpx2`
client. Tests pass in a client with a mock transport, or the API's own test client, so no server
is needed."""

from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import httpx2

from mission_control.cli.errors import AUTHENTICATION, REFUSED, UNAVAILABLE

API_PREFIX = "/api/v1"
TIMEOUT = httpx2.Timeout(15.0, connect=5.0)  # the server may wait up to 5 s for a busy database


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Problem:
    code: str
    message: str
    refs: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class ApiError(Exception):
    """The server refused the request. Carries its error body: a code, a message and every
    problem. Commands may attach a `subject` (e.g. "Mission 5"), whether a state refusal should
    name it, and hints for known codes, which are used when the refusal is shown."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        status: int,
        code: str,
        message: str,
        problems: tuple[Problem, ...] = (),
        body: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status = status
        self.code = code
        self.message = message
        self.problems = problems
        self.body: Mapping[str, Any] = body or {}
        self.subject: str | None = None
        self.name_state = False
        self.hints: dict[str, str] = {}

    @property
    def exit_code(self) -> int:
        if self.status == 401:
            return AUTHENTICATION
        if self.status == 503:
            return UNAVAILABLE
        return REFUSED

    @property
    def has_error_body(self) -> bool:
        return isinstance(self.body.get("error"), Mapping)


class Unreachable(Exception):
    def __init__(self, url: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(url)
        self.url = url


class Api:
    """Sends requests with the session token and turns error responses into `ApiError`.

    A 401 on a request that carried a token means the session is over, so
    `on_session_ended` runs (the CLI clears the saved token) before the error is raised."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        http: httpx2.Client,
        token: str | None = None,
        on_session_ended: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._http = http
        self._token = token
        self._on_session_ended = on_session_ended

    def get(self, path: str, params: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> Any:
        return self._send("GET", path, params=params)

    def post(self, path: str, body: Any = None) -> Any:
        return self._send("POST", path, body=body)

    def put(self, path: str, body: Any = None) -> Any:
        return self._send("PUT", path, body=body)

    def patch(self, path: str, body: Any = None) -> Any:
        return self._send("PATCH", path, body=body)

    def delete(self, path: str) -> Any:
        return self._send("DELETE", path)

    def _send(
        self,
        method: str,
        path: str,
        *,
        body: Any = None,
        params: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> Any:
        headers = {"Accept": "application/json"}
        if self._token:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {self._token}"
        try:
            response = self._http.request(
                method,
                API_PREFIX + path,
                json=body,
                params=dict(params) if params else None,
                headers=headers,
            )
        except httpx2.TransportError as exc:
            raise Unreachable(str(self._http.base_url).rstrip("/")) from exc

        if response.is_success:
            if response.status_code == 204 or not response.content:
                return None
            try:
                return response.json()
            except ValueError:
                raise _unexpected(response) from None

        failure = _error(response)
        if response.status_code == 401 and self._token and self._on_session_ended:
            self._on_session_ended()
        raise failure


def _error(response: httpx2.Response) -> ApiError:
    try:
        body = response.json()
    except ValueError:
        return _unexpected(response)
    error = body.get("error") if isinstance(body, dict) else None
    if not (
        isinstance(error, dict)
        and isinstance(error.get("code"), str)
        and isinstance(error.get("message"), str)
    ):
        return _unexpected(response)
    problems = tuple(
        Problem(str(p.get("code", "")), str(p.get("message", "")), p.get("refs") or {})
        for p in error.get("problems") or ()
        if isinstance(p, dict)
    )
    return ApiError(response.status_code, error["code"], error["message"], problems, body)


def _unexpected(response: httpx2.Response) -> ApiError:
    return ApiError(
        response.status_code,
        "unexpected_response",
        f"The server sent an unexpected response (HTTP {response.status_code})",
    )
"""Asking the user things. Prompts go to the terminal (stderr, or the tty for passwords), never to
stdout. Passwords are read with the input hidden, or from stdin, one line per prompt, when
there's no terminal."""

import getpass
import sys
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Protocol

from mission_control.cli.errors import Declined, UsageProblem

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from mission_control.cli.env import Env


class Prompter(Protocol):
    def ask(self, label: str) -> str: ...

    def ask_hidden(self, label: str) -> str: ...


class TerminalPrompter:
    """Reads answers from the keyboard. Ctrl+C raises KeyboardInterrupt; Ctrl+D is treated the
    same, as a way out."""

    def ask(self, label: str) -> str:
        sys.stderr.write(f"{label}: ")
        sys.stderr.flush()
        line = sys.stdin.readline()
        if not line:
            raise KeyboardInterrupt
        return line.rstrip("\r\n")

    def ask_hidden(self, label: str) -> str:
        try:
            return getpass.getpass(f"{label}: ")
        except EOFError:
            raise KeyboardInterrupt from None


def ask(env: "Env", label: str, *, missing: str) -> str:
    """A visible answer. Without a terminal, fail with `missing` instead of waiting."""
    if not env.is_interactive:
        raise UsageProblem(missing)
    while not (answer := env.prompter.ask(label).strip()):
        pass
    return answer


def ask_secret(env: "Env", label: str) -> str:
    if env.is_interactive:
        return env.prompter.ask_hidden(label)
    line = sys.stdin.readline()
    if not line:
        raise UsageProblem(
            "No password given, and there's no terminal to ask on.",
            hint="Run this in a terminal, or pipe it on stdin.",
        )
    return line.rstrip("\r\n")


def ask_new_secret(env: "Env", label: str, repeat: str, *, mismatch: str) -> str:
    """A password being set: asked twice, and both answers must match."""
    first = ask_secret(env, label)
    if first != ask_secret(env, repeat):
        raise UsageProblem(mismatch)
    return first


def confirm(env: "Env", warning: str, *, yes: bool, question: str = "Continue?") -> None:
    """Show `warning` and ask before going ahead. `--yes` skips the question. Without a terminal
    the change needs `--yes`, so a script never waits on a prompt."""
    if yes:
        return
    env.term.warn(warning)
    if not env.is_interactive:
        raise UsageProblem(
            "There's no terminal to confirm on.", hint="Re-run with --yes to go ahead."
        )
    if env.prompter.ask(f"{question} [y/N]").strip().lower() not in ("y", "yes"):
        raise Declined()
"""Failures the CLI reports itself, before or instead of asking the server, and the exit codes
every command uses."""

from collections.abc import Sequence

OK = 0
REFUSED = 1
USAGE = 2
AUTHENTICATION = 3
UNAVAILABLE = 4
INTERRUPTED = 130


class CliError(Exception):
    """Something to tell the user, with an optional next step, and the exit code to use."""

    exit_code = REFUSED

    def __init__(self, message: str, *, hint: str | None = None, bullets: Sequence[str] = ()):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.message = message
        self.hint = hint
        self.bullets = tuple(bullets)


class Refused(CliError):
    """A reference that doesn't match anything, e.g. an email nobody has."""


class Declined(CliError):
    """The user answered no to a confirmation."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__("Nothing was changed.")


class UsageProblem(CliError):
    """Input the CLI can't use, found before anything was sent."""

    exit_code = USAGE


class NotLoggedIn(CliError):
    exit_code = AUTHENTICATION

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__("You're not logged in.", hint="Log in: mc login")
```

"""Plumbing every command shares: the group and command classes (help layout, error handling),
the current `Env`, and common options."""

from datetime import date
from typing import Annotated, Any

import typer
from typer.core import TyperCommand, TyperGroup

from mission_control.cli.client import ApiError, Unreachable
from mission_control.cli.env import Env
from mission_control.cli.errors import CliError
from mission_control.cli.refusals import report


class Command(TyperCommand):
    """Usage lines that spell out the options (`mc missions match MISSION_ID [--apply]`), and
    the epilog shown as an example block."""

    def collect_usage_pieces(self, ctx: typer.Context) -> list[str]:
        pieces: list[str] = []
        options: list[str] = []
        for param in self.get_params(ctx):
            if param.param_type_name == "argument":
                pieces.append(param.make_metavar(ctx))
            elif param.param_type_name == "option" and param.name != "help" and not param.hidden:
                flag = max(param.opts, key=len)
                text = flag if param.is_flag else f"{flag} {param.make_metavar(ctx)}"
                options.append(text if param.required else f"[{text}]")
        return pieces + options

    def format_epilog(self, ctx: typer.Context, formatter: Any) -> None:
        if not self.epilog:
            return
        examples = [line.strip() for line in self.epilog.strip().splitlines() if line.strip()]
        formatter.write_paragraph()
        formatter.write_text("Example:" if len(examples) == 1 else "Examples:")
        with formatter.indentation():
            for line in examples:
                formatter.write(f"{' ' * formatter.current_indent}{line}\n")


class Group(TyperGroup):
    """A bare group prints its help and exits 0. Failures raised by commands are reported here
    with their exit code, so tests and real runs behave the same."""

    def parse_args(self, ctx: typer.Context, args: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        if not args and not ctx.resilient_parsing:
            typer.echo(ctx.get_help())
            ctx.exit(0)
        return super().parse_args(ctx, args)

    def invoke(self, ctx: typer.Context) -> Any:
        try:
            return super().invoke(ctx)
        except (CliError, ApiError, Unreachable, KeyboardInterrupt) as failure:
            env = ctx.find_object(Env) or Env()
            raise typer.Exit(report(env, failure)) from None


def group(help: str | None = None, name: str | None = None) -> typer.Typer:
    """A command group. Without `help`, its callback's docstring is the help."""
    named: dict[str, str] = {"help": help} if help else {}
    if name:
        named["name"] = name
    return typer.Typer(
        cls=Group,
        rich_markup_mode=None,
        add_completion=False,
        pretty_exceptions_enable=False,
        **named,
    )


def start(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: bool = False) -> Env:
    """The command's Env, remembering whether output is JSON so refusals print as JSON too."""
    env = ctx.ensure_object(Env)
    env.json_output = json_output
    return env


def iso_date(value: str | None) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    try:
        return date.fromisoformat(value).isoformat()
    except ValueError:
        raise typer.BadParameter(
            f"'{value}' isn't a date. Use YYYY-MM-DD, e.g. 2027-03-01."
        ) from None


Json = Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--json", help="Print the result as JSON")]
Yes = Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--yes", "-y", help="Don't ask for confirmation")]
MissionId = Annotated[int, typer.Argument(metavar="MISSION_ID", help="The mission's ID")]
Email = Annotated[str, typer.Argument(metavar="EMAIL", help="The person's email address")]


def command(app: typer.Typer, name: str, example: str | None = None, **settings: Any) -> Any:
    """Register a command with the shared help layout. `example` lines appear under Example."""
    return app.command(name, cls=Command, epilog=example, **settings)
"""Signing in and out, who am I, changing my password, and what's waiting on me."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Annotated

import typer

from mission_control.cli.client import ApiError
from mission_control.cli.commands.common import Json, command, start
from mission_control.cli.config import Credentials
from mission_control.cli.prompts import ask, ask_new_secret, ask_secret
from mission_control.cli.refusals import refusals
from mission_control.cli.render import ROLE, Terminal, plural
from mission_control.cli.views import identity, mission_row, org_name


def register(app: typer.Typer) -> None:
    command(app, "login", example="mc login --email lee@aurora.example")(login)
    command(app, "logout", example="mc logout")(logout)
    command(app, "whoami", example="mc whoami")(whoami)
    command(app, "password", example="mc password")(password)
    command(app, "inbox", example="mc inbox")(inbox)


def login(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    email: Annotated[
        str | None,
        typer.Option(
            "--email", "-e", metavar="EMAIL", help="Your email address (asked if left out)"
        ),
    ] = None,
) -> None:
    """Log in. Your password is asked for with the input hidden.

    Without a terminal, pass --email and pipe the password on stdin.
    """
    env = start(ctx)
    if email is None:
        email = ask(
            env,
            "Email",
            missing="No email given, and there's no terminal to ask on. Pass --email, and pipe "
            "the password on stdin.",
        )
    secret = ask_secret(env, "Password")
    with refusals():
        session = env.anonymous_api().post("/auth/login", {"email": email, "password": secret})
    env.store.save(Credentials(session["token"], email, session.get("expires_at")))
    me = env.api().get("/me")

    about = ROLE.get(me["role"], me["role"])
    if org := org_name(me):
        about = f"{about}, {org}"
    lasts = _session_length(session.get("expires_at"))
    env.term.ok(f"Logged in as {me['name']} ({about}).{lasts}")
    env.term.next_steps(["mc inbox"])


def logout(ctx: typer.Context) -> None:
    """Log out and end this session on the server."""
    env = start(ctx)
    if env.store.load() is None:
        env.term.ok("You're not logged in.")
        return
    try:
        env.api().post("/auth/logout")
    except ApiError as error:
        if error.status != 401:  # a session that already ended is as good as logged out
            raise
    env.store.clear()
    env.term.ok("Logged out.")


def whoami(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Show who you're logged in as."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    me = env.api().get("/me")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(me)
        return
    env.term.fields(
        identity(me) + [("Crew profile", "yes" if me.get("crew_profile_id") else "none")]
    )


def password(ctx: typer.Context) -> None:
    """Change your password. Asks for your current one, then the new one twice.

    Every session ends afterwards, including this one, so log in again.
    """
    env = start(ctx)
    api = env.api()
    current = ask_secret(env, "Current password")
    new = ask_new_secret(
        env,
        "New password",
        "Repeat new password",
        mismatch="The new passwords don't match. Nothing was changed.",
    )
    api.post("/me/password", {"current_password": current, "new_password": new})
    env.store.clear()
    env.term.ok("Password changed. All your sessions have ended, including this one.")
    env.term.next_steps(["mc login"])


def inbox(ctx: typer.Context, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Show what's waiting on you.

    Proposals to answer, missions waiting for your approval, and your missions.
    """
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    data = env.api().get("/me/inbox")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(data)
        return
    term = env.term
    proposals = data.get("assignments") or []
    approvals = data.get("approvals") or []
    missions = data.get("missions") or []
    if not (proposals or approvals or missions):
        term.ok("Nothing needs your attention.")
        return

    sections = 0
    if proposals:
        sections = _section(term, sections, "Proposals waiting for your answer")
        term.table(
            ["Assignment", "Mission", "Slot", "Dates"],
            [
                [
                    str(a["assignment_id"]),
                    a["mission_name"],
                    a["slot_name"],
                    term.dates(a["start_date"], a["end_date"]),
                ]
                for a in proposals
            ],
        )
    if approvals:
        sections = _section(term, sections, "Missions waiting for your approval")
        term.table(["ID", "Mission", "Dates"], [mission_row(term, m)[:3] for m in approvals])
    if missions:
        sections = _section(term, sections, "Your missions")
        term.table(["ID", "Mission", "Dates", "Status"], [mission_row(term, m) for m in missions])

    steps = []
    if proposals:
        first = proposals[0]["assignment_id"]
        steps += [f"mc assignments accept {first}", f"mc assignments decline {first}"]
    if approvals:
        steps.append(f"mc missions show {approvals[0]['id']}")
    term.next_steps(steps)


def _section(term: Terminal, shown: int, title: str) -> int:
    if shown:
        term.line()
    term.line(title)
    return shown + 1


def _session_length(expires_at: str | None) -> str:
    if not expires_at:
        return ""
    try:
        expires = datetime.fromisoformat(expires_at.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    except ValueError:
        return ""
    hours = round((expires - datetime.now(UTC)).total_seconds() / 3600)
    return f" Session lasts {plural(hours, 'hour')}." if hours >= 1 else ""

```
"""M16: the PRD scenarios S1 (golden path) and S4 (overlaps) driven through `mc`, one user after
another, against a small stateful stand-in for the API (PRD §9.3, AC-1, AC-3, AC-8, AC-14, AC-15).

The stand-in only keeps the state these flows touch, and answers with the design's response and
error shapes. Business rules are the server's; here they're scripted to the PRD's expected
outcomes, so the tests check what the CLI sends and how it reports each step.
"""

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import httpx2
from cli_fakes import CREW, SKILLS, USERS, FakeApi, error, gateway, ok, problem

PEOPLE = {
    "ruth@aurora.example": (1, "Ruth Adeyemi", "director", None),
    "marco@aurora.example": (2, "Marco Bianchi", "director", 2),
    "ingrid@aurora.example": (3, "Ingrid Holm", "director", None),
    "lee@aurora.example": (4, "Lee Park", "lead", None),
    "nora@aurora.example": (5, "Nora Quinn", "lead", None),
    "ana@aurora.example": (6, "Ana Okafor", "lead", 6),
    "chen@aurora.example": (8, "Chen Wu", "crew", 8),
    "dana@aurora.example": (9, "Dana Reyes", "crew", 9),
}
SLOT_NAMES = {1: "Flight Engineer", 2: "Mission Specialist", 3: "Commander"}


@dataclass
class Server:
    """Just enough of the API for S1 and S4."""

    api: FakeApi
    tokens: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    status: str = "draft"
    assignments: dict[int, dict] = field(default_factory=dict)
    approvals: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    history: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    accepted_reboost: set[int] = field(default_factory=set)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        on = self.api.on
        on("POST", "/auth/login", self.login)
        on("GET", "/me", self.authed(self.me))
        on("GET", "/skills", self.authed(lambda r, u: ok(SKILLS)))
        on("GET", "/crew", self.authed(lambda r, u: ok(CREW)))
        on("GET", "/users", self.authed(lambda r, u: ok(USERS)))
        on("GET", "/missions/1", self.authed(lambda r, u: ok(self.mission())))
        on("GET", "/missions/1/match", self.authed(self.match))
        on("POST", "/missions/1/match/apply", self.authed(self.apply))
        on("GET", "/assignments", self.authed(self.mine))
        for aid in (21, 22, 23):
            on("POST", f"/assignments/{aid}/accept", self.authed(self.accept(aid)))
        on("POST", "/missions/1/submit", self.authed(self.submit))
        on("POST", "/missions/1/approve", self.authed(self.approve))
        on("POST", "/missions/1/complete", self.authed(self.complete))
        on("GET", "/missions/1/history", self.authed(lambda r, u: ok(self.history)))

    # plumbing

    def authed(self, handler):
        def wrapped(request: httpx2.Request) -> httpx2.Response:
            auth = request.headers.get("authorization", "")
            email = self.tokens.get(auth.removeprefix("Bearer "))
            if email is None:
                return error(401, "unauthenticated", "Your session has ended")
            return handler(request, PEOPLE[email])

        return wrapped

    def login(self, request: httpx2.Request) -> httpx2.Response:
        body = json.loads(request.content)
        if body["email"] not in PEOPLE or body["password"] != "orbital launch window":
            return error(401, "invalid_credentials", "Email or password is incorrect")
        token = f"tok-{body['email'].split('@')[0]}"
        self.tokens[token] = body["email"]
        return ok({"token": token, "expires_at": "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z"})

    def me(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        uid, name, role, cpid = user
        email = next(e for e, p in PEOPLE.items() if p == user)
        return ok({"id": uid, "email": email, "name": name, "role": role, "is_active": True,
                   "crew_profile_id": cpid,
                   "organisation": {"id": 1, "name": "Aurora Space Agency"}})  # fmt: skip

    def log(self, user, event, from_status=None, to_status=None) -> None:
        self.history.append(
            {"id": len(self.history) + 1,
             "created_at": f"2027-01-05T09:{len(self.history):02d}:00Z",
             "actor_id": user[0], "actor_name": user[1], "event": event,
             "from_status": from_status, "to_status": to_status, "reason": None, "data": {}}
        )  # fmt: skip

    def mission(self) -> dict:
        open_places = {
            slot: 1 - sum(1 for a in self.assignments.values()
                          if a["slot_id"] == slot and a["status"] in ("proposed", "accepted"))
            for slot in SLOT_NAMES
        }  # fmt: skip
        m = gateway(self.status, list(self.assignments.values()), self.approvals, open_places)
        m["approvals_required"] = None if self.status == "draft" else 2
        return m

    # endpoints

    def match(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        from cli_fakes import S2_PREVIEW

        return ok(S2_PREVIEW | {"can_apply": user[0] == 4})

    def apply(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        for aid, slot, cpid, name in ((21, 3, 6, "Ana Okafor"), (22, 1, 8, "Chen Wu"),
                                      (23, 2, 9, "Dana Reyes")):  # fmt: skip
            self.assignments[aid] = {"id": aid, "slot_id": slot, "slot_name": SLOT_NAMES[slot],
                                     "crew_profile_id": cpid, "crew_name": name,
                                     "status": "proposed", "reason": None}  # fmt: skip
            self.log(user, "assignment.proposed")
        return ok(
            [{k: a[k] for k in ("id", "slot_id", "crew_profile_id", "status")}
             for a in self.assignments.values()],
            status=201,
        )  # fmt: skip

    def mine(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        rows = [
            {"assignment_id": a["id"], "mission_id": 1, "mission_name": "Lunar Gateway Resupply",
             "start_date": "2027-03-01", "end_date": "2027-03-10", "mission_status": self.status,
             "slot_name": a["slot_name"], "status": a["status"], "reason": None}
            for a in self.assignments.values() if a["crew_profile_id"] == user[3]
        ]  # fmt: skip
        if request.url.params.get("pending") == "true":
            rows = [r for r in rows if r["status"] == "proposed"]
        return ok(rows)

    def accept(self, aid):
        def handler(request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
            a = self.assignments.get(aid)
            if a is None or a["crew_profile_id"] != user[3]:
                return error(404, "not_found", "Assignment not found")
            a["status"] = "accepted"
            self.log(user, "assignment.accepted", "proposed", "accepted")
            return ok({"id": aid, "mission_id": 1, "slot_id": a["slot_id"],
                       "crew_profile_id": a["crew_profile_id"], "status": "accepted",
                       "reason": None})  # fmt: skip

        return handler

    def submit(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        waiting = [a for a in self.assignments.values() if a["status"] != "accepted"]
        if waiting:
            return error(409, "rule_violation", "Mission can't be submitted", [
                problem("proposal_unanswered",
                        f"{a['slot_name']}: {a['crew_name']} hasn't responded to the proposal",
                        assignment_id=a["id"]) for a in waiting
            ])  # fmt: skip
        self.status = "pending_approval"
        self.log(user, "mission.submitted", "draft", "pending_approval")
        return ok(self.mission())

    def approve(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        if user[0] == 1:
            return error(403, "approver_is_creator", "You can't approve a mission you created")
        self.approvals.append({"director_id": user[0], "director_name": user[1],
                               "decision": "approve", "reason": None})  # fmt: skip
        self.log(user, "mission.approved")
        if len(self.approvals) == 2:
            self.status = "active"
            self.log(user, "mission.activated", "pending_approval", "active")
        return ok(self.mission())

    def complete(self, request, user) -> httpx2.Response:
        self.status = "completed"
        for a in self.assignments.values():
            a["status"] = "completed"
        self.log(user, "mission.completed", "active", "completed")
        return ok(self.mission())


def _login(mc, email):
    result = mc("login", "--email", email, input="orbital launch window\n")
    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.stderr
    return result


def test_s1_golden_path_through_the_cli(mc, api):
    server = Server(api)

    _login(mc, "lee@aurora.example")
    preview = mc("missions", "match", "1")
    assert preview.exit_code == 0
    assert "Commander            Ana" in preview.stdout
    applied = mc("missions", "match", "1", "--apply")
    assert applied.exit_code == 0, applied.stderr
    assert "Proposed 3 crew for Lunar Gateway Resupply" in applied.stdout

    early = mc("missions", "submit", "1")
    assert early.exit_code == 1  # AC-2: proposals still unanswered
    assert "  • Commander: Ana Okafor hasn't responded to the proposal\n" in early.stderr

    for email, aid in (("ana", 21), ("chen", 22), ("dana", 23)):
        _login(mc, f"{email}@aurora.example")
import pytest
from cli_fakes import FakeApi, Mc


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _no_ambient_terminal_settings(monkeypatch):
    """The developer's own NO_COLOR, TERM or API URL must not change what the tests see."""
    for name in ("NO_COLOR", "TERM", "MC_API_URL"):
        monkeypatch.delenv(name, raising=False)


@pytest.fixture
def api() -> FakeApi:
    return FakeApi()


@pytest.fixture
def mc(api, tmp_path) -> Mc:
    return Mc(api=api, config_dir=tmp_path / "config")

"""API test setup: a freshly seeded database file per test, an in-process client against it,
and a helper that logs in as a seeded user.

Seed ids (PRD §9): a crew profile's id equals its user's id. Emails are
`<first name>@<org slug>.example`, e.g. lee@aurora.example, kai@helix.example.
"""

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from mission_control.api.app import create_app
from mission_control.infra import security
from mission_control.infra.db import connect, init_db
from mission_control.infra.settings import Settings
from mission_control.seed.data import DEMO_PASSWORD
from mission_control.seed.loader import load_seed


@pytest.fixture
def db_path(tmp_path):
    """The PRD seed, with a real (cheap in tests) Argon2 hash of the demo passphrase."""
    path = tmp_path / "mission_control.db"
    conn = connect(path)
    init_db(conn)
    load_seed(conn, password_hash=security.hash_password(DEMO_PASSWORD))
    conn.close()
    return path


@pytest.fixture
def settings(db_path):
    return Settings(database_path=db_path)


@pytest.fixture
def client(settings):
    with TestClient(create_app(settings)) as c:
        yield c


@pytest.fixture
def db(db_path):
    """A separate connection to the same file, for checking what the API wrote."""
    conn = connect(db_path)
    yield conn
    conn.close()


@pytest.fixture
def login(client):
    """`login("lee")` logs in as a seeded user and returns the request headers to use."""

    def _login(who: str, org: str | None = None, password: str = DEMO_PASSWORD) -> dict:
        email = who if "@" in who else f"{who}@{org or _org_of(who)}.example"
        response = client.post("/api/v1/auth/login", json={"email": email, "password": password})
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {response.json()['token']}"}

    return _login


_HELIX = {"sam", "priya", "kai", "lena", "omar", "tess"}


def _org_of(first_name: str) -> str:
    return "helix" if first_name in _HELIX else "aurora"


def snapshot(conn) -> dict[str, list[tuple]]:
    """Every table's rows, to prove a request changed nothing."""
    tables = [
        r[0]
        for r in conn.execute(
            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'"
        )
    ]
    return {
        t: [tuple(r) for r in conn.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {t} ORDER BY rowid")] for t in tables
    }

# Crew


@command(app, "match", example="mc missions match 1")
def match(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    mission_id: MissionId,
    apply: Annotated[
        bool, typer.Option("--apply", help="Propose the suggested crew (owner only)")
    ] = False,
    json_output: Annotated[
        bool, typer.Option("--json", help="Print the suggestion as JSON")
    ] = False,
) -> None:
    """Suggest crew for a draft mission's open places. Nothing is changed.

    Owner or Director.
    """
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    hints = {
        "mission_frozen": (
            f"Only draft missions can be matched. See its crew: mc missions show {mission_id}"
        ),
        "invalid_transition": (
            "Only draft missions can be matched. Its places are already filled: "
            f"mc missions show {mission_id}"
        ),
        "mission_final": (
            f"Only draft missions can be matched. See it: mc missions show {mission_id}"
        ),
    }
    with _refusals(mission_id, **hints):
        if apply:
            _apply(env, api, mission_id, json_output)
            return
        preview = api.get(f"/missions/{mission_id}/match")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(preview)
        return
    _show_preview(env.term, mission_id, preview)


def _show_preview(term: Terminal, mission_id: int, preview: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    mission = preview["mission"]
    term.line(
        term.heading(
            mission["name"], term.dates(mission["start_date"], mission["end_date"]), "Draft"
        )
    )
    slots = [s for s in preview["slots"] if s["open_places"] > 0]
    open_places = preview["open_places"]
    unfilled = [s for s in slots if s["unfilled"] > 0]
    suggested = sorted(
        ((p, s) for s in slots for p in s["suggested"]), key=lambda pair: pair[0]["pick"]
    )
    if open_places == 0:
        term.ok("Every place already has someone proposed or accepted. Nothing to suggest.")
        return
    if not unfilled:
        places = "the 1 open place" if open_places == 1 else f"all {open_places} open places"
        term.ok(f"Crew suggested for {places}. Nothing has been proposed yet.")
    else:
        names = join_names([s["name"] for s in unfilled])
        verb = "has" if len(unfilled) == 1 else "have"
        filled = open_places - preview["unfilled_places"]
        term.note(
            f"Crew suggested for {filled} of {plural(open_places, 'open place')}. "
            f"{names} {verb} no eligible crew left."
        )
    term.line()
    rows: list[list[str] | Span] = []
    for slot in slots:
        for person in slot["suggested"]:
            rows.append(
                [
                    slot["name"],
                    person["name"],
                    plural(person["workload"], "day"),
                    str(person["proficiency"]),
                    str(person["pick"]),
                ]
            )
        if slot["unfilled"]:
            rows.append(Span(slot["name"], f"unfilled ({plural(slot['unfilled'], 'place')})"))
    term.table(["Slot", "Suggested", "Workload", "Proficiency", "Pick"], rows)
    term.line()
    if unfilled:
        term.line("  The mission can't be submitted until every place is filled.")
    else:
        term.line(
            "  Slots with the fewest eligible crew are filled first. Within a slot, lowest workload"
        )
        term.line("  wins, then highest proficiency (sum of the required skill levels).")

    if not preview.get("can_apply"):
        return
    steps = []
    if suggested:
        if len(suggested) == 1:
            person, slot = suggested[0]
            who = f"{person['name']} for {slot['name']}"
        else:
            who = join_names([p["name"] for p, _ in suggested])
        steps.append(f"mc missions match {mission_id} --apply    proposes {who}")
    for slot in unfilled:
        steps.append(
            f"mc missions propose {mission_id} --slot {shlex.quote(slot['name'])} --crew EMAIL    "
            f"fill {slot['name']} by hand"
        )
    term.next_steps(steps)


def _apply(env: Env, api: Api, mission_id: int, json_output: bool) -> None:
    proposed = api.post(f"/missions/{mission_id}/match/apply")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(proposed)
        return
    if not proposed:
        env.term.note("No crew could be proposed: no eligible crew for the open places.")
        return
    mission = api.get(f"/missions/{mission_id}")
    by_id = {a["id"]: a for a in mission.get("assignments") or []}
    env.term.ok(
        f"Proposed {len(proposed)} crew for {mission['name']}. "
        f"{'They need' if len(proposed) == 1 else 'Each of them needs'} to accept."
    )
    env.term.line()
    rows = []
    for item in proposed:
        known = by_id.get(item["id"], {})
        rows.append([str(item["id"]), known.get("crew_name", ""), known.get("slot_name", "")])
    env.term.table(["Assignment", "Crew", "Slot"], rows)
    env.term.next_steps([f"mc missions show {mission_id}"])


@command(
    app,
    "propose",
    example='mc missions propose 1 --slot "Flight Engineer" --crew chen@aurora.example',
)
def propose(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    mission_id: MissionId,
    slot: Annotated[
        str, typer.Option("--slot", metavar="SLOT", help="The slot's name or position")
    ],
    crew: Annotated[str, typer.Option("--crew", metavar="EMAIL", help="The crew member's email")],
    yes: Yes = False,
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Propose a crew member for a slot. Owner only. They still need to accept."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    free = f"See who is free: mc missions match {mission_id}"
    hints = {
        "overlap": free,
        "unavailable": free,
        "skill_below_minimum": free,
        "inactive": free,
        "slot_full": f"See the open places: mc missions show {mission_id}",
    }
    with _refusals(mission_id, **hints):
        mission = api.get(f"/missions/{mission_id}")
        target = slot_in(mission, slot)
        person = crew_by_email(api, crew)
        was_pending = _confirm_if_pending(env, mission, "Proposing crew", yes)
        assignment = api.post(
            f"/missions/{mission_id}/assignments",
            {"slot_id": target["id"], "crew_profile_id": person["crew_profile_id"]},
        )
    _done(
        env,
        json_output,
        assignment,
        f"Proposed {person['name']} for {target['name']} on {mission['name']} "
        f"(assignment {assignment['id']}). They need to accept.{_back(was_pending)}",
    )


@command(app, "remove-crew", example="mc missions remove-crew 1 21")
def remove_crew(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    mission_id: MissionId,
    assignment_id: Annotated[
        int, typer.Argument(metavar="ASSIGNMENT_ID", help="From mc missions show")
    ],
    yes: Yes = False,
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Remove someone from a mission, or take back a proposal. Owner only."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    with _refusals(mission_id):
        mission = api.get(f"/missions/{mission_id}")
        held = next((a for a in mission.get("assignments") or [] if a["id"] == assignment_id), None)
        if held is None:
            raise Refused(
                f"Assignment {assignment_id} isn't on {mission['name']}",
                hint=f"See its crew: mc missions show {mission_id}",
            )
        was_pending = _confirm_if_pending(env, mission, "Removing crew", yes)
        result = api.post(f"/assignments/{assignment_id}/remove")
    _done(
        env,
        json_output,
        result,
        f"Removed {held['crew_name']} from {held['slot_name']} on {mission['name']}."
        f"{_back(was_pending)}",
    )


# Lifecycle


@command(app, "submit", example="mc missions submit 1")
def submit(ctx: typer.Context, mission_id: MissionId, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Submit a fully crewed draft for approval. Owner only."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    hints = {
        "slot_unfilled": f"Fill the open places: mc missions match {mission_id}",
        "proposal_unanswered": f"See who hasn't answered: mc missions show {mission_id}",
    }
    with _refusals(mission_id, **hints):
        mission = env.api().post(f"/missions/{mission_id}/submit")
    needs = mission.get("approvals_required")
    after = f" It needs {plural(needs, 'approval')}." if needs else ""
    _done(env, json_output, mission, f"Submitted {mission['name']} for approval.{after}")


@command(app, "approve", example="mc missions approve 1")
def approve(ctx: typer.Context, mission_id: MissionId, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Approve a mission pending approval. Directors who didn't create it and aren't on it."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    with _refusals(mission_id):
        mission = env.api().post(f"/missions/{mission_id}/approve")
    if mission["status"] == "active":
        state = "It's now active."
    else:
        given = sum(1 for a in mission.get("approvals") or [] if a["decision"] == "approve")
        needed = plural(mission["approvals_required"], "approval")
        state = f"It's pending approval ({given} of {needed})."
    _done(env, json_output, mission, f"Approved {mission['name']}. {state}")


@command(app, "reject", example='mc missions reject 6 --reason "Roster too thin"')
def reject(
    ctx: typer.Context, mission_id: MissionId, reason: Reason, json_output: Json = False
) -> None:
    """Reject a mission pending approval. It goes back to Draft. Directors only."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    with _refusals(mission_id):
        mission = env.api().post(f"/missions/{mission_id}/reject", {"reason": reason})
    _done(env, json_output, mission, f"Rejected {mission['name']}. It's back in Draft.")


@command(app, "transfer", example="mc missions transfer 1 --owner nora@aurora.example")
def transfer(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    mission_id: MissionId,
    owner: Annotated[str, typer.Option("--owner", metavar="EMAIL", help="The new owning Lead")],
    yes: Yes = False,
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Hand a mission to another Lead. Directors only."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    with _refusals(mission_id):
        new_owner = user_by_email(api, owner)
        mission = api.get(f"/missions/{mission_id}")
        was_pending = _confirm_if_pending(env, mission, "Changing the owner", yes)
        updated = api.post(f"/missions/{mission_id}/owner", {"owner_id": new_owner["id"]})
    _done(
        env,
        json_output,
        updated,
        f"{updated['name']} now belongs to {new_owner['name']}.{_back(was_pending)}",
    )


@command(app, "complete", example="mc missions complete 5")
def complete(ctx: typer.Context, mission_id: MissionId, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Mark an active mission completed. Owner or Director."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    with _refusals(mission_id):
        mission = env.api().post(f"/missions/{mission_id}/complete")
    _done(
        env,
        json_output,
        mission,
        f"Completed {mission['name']}. Its accepted assignments are completed too.",
    )


@command(app, "cancel", example='mc missions cancel 1 --reason "Budget cut"')
def cancel(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    mission_id: MissionId,
    reason: Reason,
    yes: Yes = False,
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """Cancel a mission that isn't finished. Owner or Director. Asks first."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    with _refusals(mission_id):
        mission = api.get(f"/missions/{mission_id}")
        confirm(
            env,
            f"Cancelling {mission['name']} can't be undone. Its open assignments will be "
            "cancelled too.",
            yes=yes,
            question=f"Cancel {mission['name']}?",
        )
        cancelled = api.post(f"/missions/{mission_id}/cancel", {"reason": reason})
    _done(env, json_output, cancelled, f"Cancelled {cancelled['name']}.")
"""Your assignments: list them, and accept, decline or withdraw."""

from contextlib import AbstractContextManager
from typing import Annotated, Any

import typer

from mission_control.cli.client import Api
from mission_control.cli.commands.common import Json, Yes, command, group, start
from mission_control.cli.prompts import confirm
from mission_control.cli.refusals import refusals
from mission_control.cli.views import assignment_summary_rows

app = group("Your assignments: accept, decline or withdraw.")

AssignmentId = Annotated[
    int, typer.Argument(metavar="ASSIGNMENT_ID", help="From mc assignments list or mc inbox")
]


def _refusals(assignment_id: int) -> AbstractContextManager[None]:
    return refusals(
        f"Assignment {assignment_id}", not_found="See your assignments: mc assignments list"
    )


def _mine(api: Api, assignment_id: int) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Your summary of the assignment (mission, slot, mission status), if it's yours."""
    for row in api.get("/assignments"):
        if row["assignment_id"] == assignment_id:
            return row
    return None


def _what(summary: dict[str, Any] | None, assignment_id: int) -> str:
    if summary is None:
        return f"assignment {assignment_id}"
    return f"{summary['slot_name']} on {summary['mission_name']}"


@command(app, "list", example="mc assignments list --pending")
def list_assignments(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    pending: Annotated[
        bool, typer.Option("--pending", help="Only proposals waiting for your answer")
    ] = False,
    json_output: Json = False,
) -> None:
    """List your assignments."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    rows = env.api().get("/assignments", {"pending": "true"} if pending else None)
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(rows)
        return
    if not rows:
        env.term.line(
            "No proposals are waiting for you." if pending else "You have no assignments."
        )
        return
    env.term.table(
        ["ID", "Mission", "Slot", "Dates", "Status"], assignment_summary_rows(env.term, rows)
    )


@command(app, "accept", example="mc assignments accept 21")
def accept(ctx: typer.Context, assignment_id: AssignmentId, json_output: Json = False) -> None:
    """Accept a proposal. Only while the mission is a draft."""
    env = start(ctx, json_output)
    api = env.api()
    with _refusals(assignment_id):
        summary = _mine(api, assignment_id)
        result = api.post(f"/assignments/{assignment_id}/accept")
    if json_output:
        env.term.json(result)
        return
    env.term.ok(f"Accepted {_what(summary, assignment_id)}.")

def _suggested(person: CandidateFacts, slot: SlotSpec, pick: int) -> SuggestedCrew:
    return SuggestedCrew(
        crew_profile_id=person.crew_profile_id,
        name=person.name,
        workload=person.workload,
        proficiency=proficiency(person, slot),
        pick=pick,
    )


def apply(ctx: ServiceContext, mission_id: int) -> list[Assignment]:
    """Propose the suggested crew for a draft mission's open places, in pick order. Only the
    owner may. Places with no candidate stay empty; with nothing to suggest, nothing changes."""
    with ctx.write() as repos:
        mission, _ = _open(ctx, repos, mission_id, Action.APPLY_MATCH)
        _, _, suggestion = _suggest(repos, mission)
        made = []
        for placement in suggestion.placements:
            # Fresh slots each time, so a proposal sees the places the previous ones took.
            slots = {s.id: s for s in repos.facts.slot_specs(mission.id)}
            profile = found(repos.crew.get(placement.crew_profile_id), "Crew member")
            made.append(
                make_proposal(ctx, repos, mission, slots, slots[placement.slot_id], profile)
            )
        return made


def _open(
    ctx: ServiceContext, repos: Repos, mission_id: int, action: Action
) -> tuple[Mission, Context]:
    """Find the mission, check the actor may preview or apply, then that it's a draft."""
    mission = visible_mission(ctx, repos, mission_id)
    about = mission_context(repos, mission)
    ctx.authorize(action, about)
    if problem := state_problem(mission.status, action):
        raise StateConflict(problem)
    return mission, about


def _suggest(
    repos: Repos, mission: Mission
) -> tuple[list[SlotSpec], list[CandidateFacts], Suggestion]:
    """The mission's slots, the candidates for it, and the matcher's suggestion from them."""
    left_out = {a.crew_profile_id for a in repos.missions.assignments(mission.id, _LEFT_OUT)}
    candidates = [
        c
        for c in repos.facts.candidates(excluding_mission_id=mission.id)
        if c.crew_profile_id not in left_out
    ]
    slots = repos.facts.slot_specs(mission.id)
    return slots, candidates, suggest_roster(slots, candidates, mission.window)
```

"""M7: the seed reproduces PRD §9 exactly (people, skills, missions, states, id order)."""

import pytest

from mission_control.seed.data import DEMO_PASSWORD
from mission_control.seed.loader import load_seed


def rows(conn, sql, *args):
    return [tuple(r) for r in conn.execute(sql, args)]


def one(conn, sql, *args):
    return conn.execute(sql, args).fetchone()[0]


def test_two_organisations_with_their_approval_counts(seeded_db):
    assert rows(seeded_db, "SELECT slug, required_approvals FROM organisations ORDER BY id") == [
        ("aurora", 2),
        ("helix", 1),
    ]


def test_people_and_roles_in_prd_table_order(seeded_db):
    aurora = rows(seeded_db, "SELECT id, name, role FROM users WHERE org_id = 1 ORDER BY id")
    assert [(i, n.split()[0], r) for i, n, r in aurora] == [
        (1, "Ruth", "director"),
        (2, "Marco", "director"),
        (3, "Ingrid", "director"),
        (4, "Lee", "lead"),
        (5, "Nora", "lead"),
        (6, "Ana", "lead"),
        (7, "Ben", "crew"),
        (8, "Chen", "crew"),
        (9, "Dana", "crew"),
        (10, "Eli", "crew"),
        (11, "Fay", "crew"),
        (12, "Gus", "crew"),
        (13, "Hana", "crew"),
    ]
    helix = rows(seeded_db, "SELECT name, role FROM users WHERE org_id = 2 ORDER BY id")
    assert [(n.split()[0], r) for n, r in helix] == [
        ("Sam", "director"),
        ("Priya", "lead"),
        ("Kai", "crew"),
        ("Lena", "crew"),
        ("Omar", "crew"),
        ("Tess", "crew"),
    ]


def test_emails_follow_firstname_at_org_example(seeded_db):
    assert one(seeded_db, "SELECT email FROM users WHERE id = 4") == "lee@aurora.example"
    assert one(seeded_db, "SELECT email FROM users WHERE name LIKE 'Sam%'") == "sam@helix.example"


def test_crew_profiles_use_their_users_id_so_the_tie_break_follows_table_order(seeded_db):
    # The matcher's last tie-break is crew_profiles.id; tests/builders.py uses the same ids.
    profiles = rows(
        seeded_db,
    expires_at: datetime
    user: User


def _now(now: datetime | None) -> datetime:
    return now or datetime.now(UTC)


def login(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    email: str,
    password: str,
    *,
    now: datetime | None = None,
    lifetime: timedelta = SESSION_LIFETIME,
) -> Session:
    """Unknown email, wrong password and deactivated user all get the same refusal, so the
    response never reveals which accounts exist."""
    now = _now(now)
    # Argon2 is slow, so it runs before the write lock is taken; the transaction then checks
    # that nothing changed since the verify.
    found = security.find_login(conn, email)
    if found is None:
        security.verify_password(security.dummy_hash(), password)  # same cost as a real check
        raise _BAD_LOGIN
    verified_hash = found["password_hash"]
    if not security.verify_password(verified_hash, password):
        raise _BAD_LOGIN
    if not found["is_active"]:
        raise _BAD_LOGIN
    upgraded_hash = (
        security.hash_password(password) if security.needs_rehash(verified_hash) else None
    )
    token = security.new_token()
    expires = now + lifetime
    with write_tx(conn):
        current = security.login_state(conn, found["id"])
        if current is None or current["password_hash"] != verified_hash:
            raise _BAD_LOGIN
        if not current["is_active"]:
            raise _BAD_LOGIN
        if upgraded_hash is not None:
            security.set_password_hash(conn, found["id"], upgraded_hash)
        security.store_token(conn, found["id"], security.hash_token(token), now, expires)
        user = Repos(conn, found["org_id"]).users.get(found["id"])
    assert user is not None
    return Session(token=token, expires_at=expires, user=user)


def authenticate(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection, token: str | None, *, now: datetime | None = None

a leftover log must not be replayed into the new file\n\n    result = admin(\"seed\", \"--reset\")\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.stderr\n    assert count(db_path, \"users\") == 19\n\n\ndef test_m17_seed_reset_works_when_there_is_no_database_yet(admin, db_path):\n    result = admin(\"seed\", \"--reset\")\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.stderr\n    assert f\"Loaded the demo data into {db_path.resolve()}.\" in result.stdout\n    assert count(db_path, \"users\") == 19\n\n\n# --- create-org ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef create_orion(admin, *extra, input=PASSPHRASE + \"\\n\"):\n    return admin(\n        \"create-org\",\n        \"--name\",\n        \"Orion Labs\",\n        \"--director-email\",\n        \"zoe@orion.example\",\n        \"--director-name\",\n        \"Zoe Lin\",\n        *extra,\n        input=input,\n    )\n\n\ndef test_t3_create_org_makes_an_organisation_and_its_first_director(admin, db_path):\n    result = create_orion(admin)\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.stderr\n    assert result.stdout.startswith(\n        \"Created Orion Labs with Zoe Lin (zoe@orion.example) as its first Director.\\n\"\n    )\n    assert \"Missions need 1 approval\" in result.stdout\n    assert \"mc login --email zoe@orion.example\" in result.stderr\n    assert query(db_path, \"SELECT name, slug, required_approvals FROM organisations\") == [\n        (\"Orion Labs\", \"orion-labs\", 1)\n    ]\n    assert query(db_path, \"SELECT email, name, role, is_active FROM users\") == [\n        (\"zoe@orion.example\", \"Zoe Lin\", \"director\", 1)\n    ]\n    # Directors get a crew profile only when they ask for one; the catalogue starts empty.\n    assert count(db_path, \"crew_profiles\") == 0\n    assert count(db_path, \"skills\") == 0\n\n\ndef test_t3_the_new_director_can_log_in_with_their_password(admin, db_path):\n    create_orion(admin)\n    stored = query(db_path, \"SELECT password_hash FROM users\")[0][0]\n    assert stored.startswith(\"$argon2id$\") and PASSPHRASE not in stored\n\n    conn = connect(db_path)\n    try:\n        session = auth.login(conn, \"zoe@orion.example\", PASSPHRASE)\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n    assert session.user.role == \"director\"\n\n\ndef test_t3_create_org_sits_alongside_the_demo_data(admin, db_path):\n    admin(\"seed\")\n    result = create_orion(admin)\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.stderr\n    assert count(db_path, \"organisations\") == 3\n    org_id = query(db_path, \"SELECT org_id FROM users WHERE email = 'zoe@orion.example'\")[0][0]\n    assert org_id not in (1, 2)\n\n\ndef test_t3_create_org_asks_for_the_password_twice_with_input_hidden(admin, monkeypatch):\n    asked = []\n\n    def getpass(prompt):\n        asked.append(prompt)\n        return PASSPHRASE\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(admin_cli, \"_is_interactive\", lambda: True)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(admin_cli.getpass, \"getpass\", getpass)\n\n    result = create_orion(admin, input=\"\")\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.stderr\n    assert asked == [\"Password for Zoe Lin: \", \"Repeat the password: \"]\n\n\ndef test_t3_passwords_that_dont_match_change_nothing(admin, db_path, monkeypatch):\n    answers = iter([PASSPHRASE, PASSPHRASE + \"!\"])\n    monkeypatch.setattr(admin_cli, \"_is_interactive\", lambda: True)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(admin_cli.getpass, \"getpass\", lambda prompt: next(answers))\n\n    result = create_orion(admin, input=\"\")\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 2\n    assert result.stderr == \"Error: The passwords don't match. Nothing was changed.\\n\"\n    assert count(db_path, \"organisations\") == 0\n\n\ndef test_t3_ctrl_c_at_the_password_prompt_changes_nothing(admin, db_path, monkeypatch):\n    def interrupted(prompt):\n        raise KeyboardInterrupt\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(admin_cli, \"_is_interactive\", lambda: True)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(admin_cli.getpass, \"getpass\", interrupted)\n\n    result = create_orion(admin, input=\"\")\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 130\n    assert \"Nothing was changed\" in result.stderr\n    assert count(db_path, \"organisations\") == 0\n    assert_no_traceback(result)\n\n\ndef test_t3_without_a_terminal_or_a_piped_password_it_says_how(admin, db_path):\n    result = create_orion(admin, input=\"\")\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 2\n    assert \"No password given, and there's no terminal to ask on.\" in result.stderr\n    assert \"pipe the password on stdin\" in result.stderr\n    assert count(db_path, \"organisations\") == 0\n\n\ndef test_t3_the_password_is_never_a_flag(admin, db_path):\n    result = create_orion(admin, \"--password\", PASSPHRASE)\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 2\n    assert count(db_path, \"organisations\") == 0\n\n\ndef test_p10_a_password_shorter_than_15_characters_is_refused(admin, db_path):\n    result = create_orion(admin, input=\"fourteen chars\\n\")\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 2\n    assert result.stderr.startswith(\n        \"Error: The password must be at least 15 characters. Nothing was changed.\\n\"\n    )\n    assert count(db_path, \"organisations\") == 0\n\n\ndef test_p10_a_15_character_passphrase_is_enough(admin):\n    result = create_orion(admin, input=\"fifteen chars!!\\n\")\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.stderr\n\n\ndef test_t3_an_email_already_in_use_is_refused_before_asking_for_a_password(\n    admin, db_path, monkeypatch\n):\n    admin(\"seed\")\n    monkeypatch.setattr(admin_cli, \"_is_interactive\", lambda: True)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(admin_cli.getpass, \"getpass\", lambda prompt: pytest.fail(\"asked\"))\n\n    result = admin(\n        \"create-org\",\n        \"--name\",\n        \"Orion Labs\",\n        \"--director-email\",\n        \"LEE@aurora.example\",\n        \"--director-name\",\n        \"Lee Again\",\n    )\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 1\n    assert result.stderr.startswith(\n        \"Error: The email address LEE@aurora.example is already in use. Nothing was changed.\\n\"\n    )\n    assert count(db_path, \"organisations\") == 2\n\n\ndef test_t3_an_organisation_with_the_same_name_is_refused(admin, db_path):\n    create_orion(admin)\n    result = admin(\n        \"create-org\",\n        \"--name\",\n        \"orion  LABS\",\n        \"--director-email\",\n        \"max@orion.example\",\n        \"--director-name\",\n        \"Max Lin\",\n        input=PASSPHRASE + \"\\n\",\n    )\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 1\n    assert result.stderr.startswith(\n        \"Error: An organisation called Orion Labs already exists. Nothing was changed.\\n\"\n    )\n    assert count(db_path, \"organisations\") == 1\n    assert count(db_path, \"users\") == 1\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\n    \"flag, value, message\",\n    [\n        (\"--name\", \"   \", \"The organisation needs a name.\"),\n        (\"--name\", \"!!!\", \"The organisation's name needs at least one letter or number.\"),\n        (\"--director-name\", \" \", \"The Director needs a name.\"),\n        (\"--director-email\", \" \", \"The Director needs an email address.\"),\n    ],\n)\ndef test_t3_blank_values_are_usage_errors(admin, db_path, flag, value, message):\n    values = {\n        \"--name\": \"Orion Labs\",\n        \"--director-email\": \"zoe@orion.example\",\n        \"--director-name\": \"Zoe Lin\",\n    }\n    values[flag] = value\n    args = [part for pair in values.items() for part in pair]\n\n    result = admin(\"create-org\", *args, input=PASSPHRASE + \"\\n\")\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 2\n    assert result.stderr == f\"Error: {message}\\n\"\n    assert not db_path.exists() or count(db_path, \"organisations\") == 0\n\n\ndef test_t3_missing_flags_are_usage_errors(admin):\n    result = admin(\"create-org\", \"--name\", \"Orion Labs\", input=PASSPHRASE + \"\\n\")\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 2\n    assert \"--director-email\" in result.stderr\n\n\ndef test_t3_names_and_emails_are_trimmed(admin, db_path):\n    result = admin(\n        \"create-org\",\n        \"--name\",\n        \"  Orion Labs \",\n        \"--director-email\",\n        \" zoe@orion.example \",\n        \"--director-name\",\n        \" Zoe Lin \",\n        input=PASSPHRASE + \"\\n\",\n    )\n\n    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.stderr\n    assert query(db_path, \"SELECT name FROM organisations\") == [(\"Orion Labs\",)]\n    assert query(db_path, \"SELECT email, name FROM users\") == [(\"zoe@orion.example\", \"Zoe Lin\")]\n"
}
