```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/pritanbarai/Desktop/JOBS/mutinex_mission_control/.worktrees/m17-readme/src/mission_control/admin_cli.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"`mc-admin`: operator commands for the machine that runs the server. They work on the database\nfile directly rather than through the API: set up the schema, load the demo data, and create an\norganisation with its first Director (there is no self-service signup).\"\"\"\n\nimport getpass\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport sys\nimport unicodedata\nfrom collections.abc import Generator\nfrom contextlib import contextmanager\nfrom importlib.metadata import PackageNotFoundError, version\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Annotated, Any\n\nimport typer\nfrom typer.core import TyperGroup\n\nfrom mission_control.domain.enums import Role\nfrom mission_control.domain.passwords import (\n    MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH,\n    PasswordTooShort,\n    validate_password,\n)\nfrom mission_control.infra import security\nfrom mission_control.infra.db import DatabaseBusy, UnsupportedSQLite, connect, init_db, write_tx\nfrom mission_control.infra.repositories import Repos\nfrom mission_control.infra.settings import Settings\nfrom mission_control.seed import data\nfrom mission_control.seed.loader import load_seed\n\nOK, FAILED, USAGE, INTERRUPTED = 0, 1, 2, 130\n_START_OVER = \"Start over with only the demo data: mc-admin seed --reset (deletes everything in it)\"\n\n\nclass Failure(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Something to tell the operator, with an optional next step, and the exit code to use.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, message: str, *, hint: str | None = None, exit_code: int = FAILED):\n        super().__init__(message)\n        self.message = message\n        self.hint = hint\n        self.exit_code = exit_code\n\n\nclass _Group(TyperGroup):\n    \"\"\"A bare `mc-admin` prints its help and exits 0. Failures are reported in words with their\n    exit code, never as a traceback.\"\"\"\n\n    def parse_args(self, ctx: typer.Context, args: list[str]) -> list[str]:\n        if not args and not ctx.resilient_parsing:\n            typer.echo(ctx.get_help())\n            ctx.exit(OK)\n        return super().parse_args(ctx, args)\n\n    def invoke(self, ctx: typer.Context) -> Any:\n        try:\n            return super().invoke(ctx)\n        except Failure as failure:\n            _error(failure.message, failure.hint)\n            raise typer.Exit(failure.exit_code) from None\n        except KeyboardInterrupt:\n            _error(\"Interrupted. Nothing was changed.\")\n            raise typer.Exit(INTERRUPTED) from None\n\n\napp = typer.Typer(\n    cls=_Group,\n    name=\"mc-admin\",\n    rich_markup_mode=None,\n    add_completion=False,\n    pretty_exceptions_enable=False,\n)\n\n\ndef _example(*lines: str) -> str:\n    heading = \"Example:\" if len(lines) == 1 else \"Examples:\"\n    # \\b stops the help formatter from re-wrapping the lines.\n    return \"\\b\\n\" + heading + \"\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(f\"  {line}\" for line in lines)\n\n\ndef _show_version(value: bool) -> None:\n    if value:\n        try:\n            number = version(\"mission-control\")\n        except PackageNotFoundError:\n            number = \"unknown\"\n        typer.echo(f\"mc-admin {number}\")\n        raise typer.Exit()\n\n\n@app.callback()\ndef root(\n    show_version: Annotated[\n        bool,\n        typer.Option(\n            \"--version\", help=\"Show the version and exit\", callback=_show_version, is_eager=True\n        ),\n    ] = False,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Operator commands for Mission Control.\n\n    They work on the database file directly, so run them on the machine that runs mc-server.\n    The file is MC_DATABASE_PATH, or mission_control.db in the current folder.\n\n    Exit codes: 0 OK, 1 refused or failed, 2 usage, 130 interrupted.\n    \"\"\"\n\n\n# --- Commands -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@app.command(\"init-db\", epilog=_example(\"mc-admin init-db\"))\ndef init_database() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create an empty database with the current schema. Safe to run again.\"\"\"\n    settings = _settings()\n    path = _shown(settings.database_path)\n    with _database(settings) as conn:\n        created = _prepare(conn, path)\n    if created:\n        typer.echo(f\"Created an empty database at {path}.\")\n        _next_steps([\"mc-admin seed\", \"mc-admin create-org --help\"])\n    else:\n        typer.echo(f\"The database at {path} is already set up.\")\n\n\n@app.command(\"seed\", epilog=_example(\"mc-admin seed\", \"mc-admin seed --reset\"))\ndef seed(\n    reset: Annotated[\n        bool,\n        typer.Option(\"--reset\", help=\"Delete the database file first, and everything in it\"),\n    ] = False,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Load the demo organisations, people and missions.\n\n    Every demo user's password is the demo passphrase. Without --reset, a database that already\n    has data is left as it is. Stop mc-server before using --reset.\n    \"\"\"\n    settings = _settings()\n    path = _shown(settings.database_path)\n    password_hash = security.hash_password(data.DEMO_PASSWORD)  # slow, so before any lock\n    replaced = _delete_database(settings.database_path) if reset else False\n    with _database(settings) as conn:\n        _prepare(conn, path)\n        try:\n            load_seed(conn, password_hash)\n        except RuntimeError:  # the database already has organisations\n            raise Failure(\n                f\"The database at {path} already has data. Nothing was changed.\",\n                hint=_START_OVER,\n            ) from None\n\n    if replaced:\n        typer.echo(f\"Replaced the database at {path} with the demo data.\")\n    else:\n        typer.echo(f\"Loaded the demo data into {path}.\")\n    users = len(data.USERS)\n    typer.echo(\n        f\"  {len(data.ORGANISATIONS)} organisations, {users} users and {len(data.MISSIONS)} \"\n        f'missions. Every user\\'s password is \"{data.DEMO_PASSWORD}\".'\n    )\n    _next_steps([\"mc-server\", \"mc login --email lee@aurora.example\"])\n\n\n@app.command(\n    \"create-org\",\n    epilog=_example(\n        'mc-admin create-org --name \"Orion Labs\" --director-email zoe@orion.example '\n        '--director-name \"Zoe Lin\"',\n    ),\n)\ndef create_org(\n    name: Annotated[str, typer.Option(\"--name\", metavar=\"NAME\", help=\"The organisation's name\")],\n    director_email: Annotated[\n        str,\n        typer.Option(\"--director-email\", metavar=\"EMAIL\", help=\"The first Director's email\"),\n    ],\n    director_name: Annotated[\n        str,\n        typer.Option(\"--director-name\", metavar=\"NAME\", help=\"The first Director's full name\"),\n    ],\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create an organisation and its first Director.\n\n    The Director's password is asked for twice with the input hidden. Without a terminal, pipe\n    it on stdin. It must be at least 15 characters; passphrases are welcome.\n    \"\"\"\n    org_name = _required(name, \"The organisation needs a name.\")\n    slug = _slug(org_name)\n    if not slug:\n        raise Failure(\n            \"The organisation's name needs at least one letter or number.\", exit_code=USAGE\n        )\n    email = _required(director_email, \"The Director needs an email address.\")\n    person = _required(director_name, \"The Director needs a name.\")\n\n    settings = _settings()\n    path = _shown(settings.database_path)\n    with _database(settings) as conn:\n        _prepare(conn, path)\n        _refuse_if_taken(conn, slug, email)  # before asking, so no password is typed for nothing\n        password = _new_password(person)\n        try:\n            validate_password(password)\n        except PasswordTooShort:\n            raise Failure(\n                f\"The password must be at least {MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH} characters. \"\n                \"Nothing was changed.\",\n                hint=\"A few words make a good passphrase. Run the command again.\",\n                exit_code=USAGE,\n            ) from None\n        password_hash = security.hash_password(password)  # slow, so before the write lock\n        with write_tx(conn):\n            _refuse_if_taken(conn, slug, email)  # again, now that nothing else can write\n            org_id = _insert_organisation(conn, org_name, slug)\n            Repos(conn, org_id).users.create(email, person, Role.DIRECTOR, password_hash)\n\n    typer.echo(f\"Created {org_name} with {person} ({email}) as its first Director.\")\n    typer.echo(\n        f\"  Missions need 1 approval to go active. {person.split()[0]} can change that with \"\n        \"mc org set-approvals.\"\n    )\n    _next_steps(\n        [\n            f\"mc login --email {email}\",\n            \"mc skills add NAME\",\n            \"mc users add --email EMAIL --name NAME --role lead\",\n        ]\n    )\n\n\n# --- Database -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _settings() -> Settings:\n    import os\n\n    try:\n        return Settings.from_env(os.environ)\n    except ValueError as e:\n        raise Failure(f\"{e}.\", exit_code=USAGE) from None\n\n\ndef _shown(path: Path) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"The database path as the operator should see it: absolute, so there's no doubt which\n    file was used.\"\"\"\n    return path.expanduser().resolve()\n\n\n@contextmanager\ndef _database(settings: Settings) -> Generator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    \"\"\"A connection to the configured file, with database failures put into words.\"\"\"\n    path = _shown(settings.database_path)\n    try:\n        conn = connect(settings.database_path, busy_timeout_ms=settings.busy_timeout_ms)\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        raise Failure(\n            f\"Can't open the database at {path}.\",\n            hint=\"Check that its folder exists, or set MC_DATABASE_PATH to another file.\",\n        ) from None\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    except DatabaseBusy:\n        raise Failure(\n            f\"The database at {path} is busy. Nothing was changed.\",\n            hint=\"Try again in a moment.\",\n        ) from None\n    except sqlite3.DatabaseError as e:\n        if type(e) is not sqlite3.DatabaseError:  # constraint failures etc. are real bugs\n            raise\n        raise Failure(\n            f\"{path} isn't a Mission Control database. Nothing was changed.\",\n            hint=\"Set MC_DATABASE_PATH to another file, or replace it: mc-admin seed --reset\",\n        ) from None\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef _prepare(conn: sqlite3.Connection, path: Path) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Make sure the schema is there. True if it was created just now.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        empty = conn.execute(\"PRAGMA user_version\").fetchone()[0] == 0\n        init_db(conn)\n    except UnsupportedSQLite as e:\n        raise Failure(f\"{e}.\", hint=\"Use a Python built with a newer SQLite.\") from None\n    except RuntimeError as e:  # a schema version this code doesn't know\n        raise Failure(\n            f\"The database at {path} can't be used: {e}.\",\n            hint=\"Set MC_DATABASE_PATH to another file, or \" + _START_OVER[0].lower()\n            + _START_OVER[1:],\n        ) from None\n    return empty\n\n\ndef _delete_database(path: Path) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Remove the database file and SQLite's side files, so a stale log can't be replayed into\n    the new one. True if there was a database to delete.\"\"\"\n    existed = path.exists()\n    for victim in (path, *(path.with_name(path.name + s) for s in (\"-wal\", \"-shm\", \"-journal\"))):\n        try:\n            victim.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n        except OSError as e:\n            raise Failure(\n                f\"Can't delete {_shown(victim)}: {e.strerror}.\",\n                hint=\"Stop mc-server and check the file's permissions, then try again.\",\n            ) from None\n    return existed\n\n\ndef _refuse_if_taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, email: str) -> None:\n    existing = _organisation_with_slug(conn, slug)\n    if existing is not None:\n        raise Failure(f\"An organisation called {existing} already exists. Nothing was changed.\")\n    # Emails are unique across every organisation, because login is by email alone.\n    if security.find_login(conn, email) is not None:\n        raise Failure(\n            f\"The email address {email} is already in use. Nothing was changed.\",\n            hint=\"Use another email address for this organisation's Director.\",\n        )\n\n\n# Organisations are created only here and by the seed, before any tenant exists to scope by.\n\n\ndef _organisation_with_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str) -> str | None:\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT name FROM organisations WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone()\n    return row[0] if row else None\n\n\ndef _insert_organisation(conn: sqlite3.Connection, name: str, slug: str) -> int:\n    cursor = conn.execute(\"INSERT INTO organisations(name, slug) VALUES (?, ?)\", (name, slug))\n    assert cursor.lastrowid is not None\n    return cursor.lastrowid\n\n\n# --- Input and output ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _required(value: str, message: str) -> str:\n    value = value.strip()\n    if not value:\n        raise Failure(message, exit_code=USAGE)\n    return value\n\n\ndef _slug(name: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A name's short form, e.g. \"Orion Labs\" → \"orion-labs\". Names that differ only in case,\n    spacing or punctuation give the same slug, so they count as the same organisation.\"\"\"\n    plain = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", name).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", plain.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n\n\ndef _is_interactive() -> bool:\n    try:\n        return sys.stdin.isatty()\n    except (AttributeError, ValueError):\n        return False\n\n\ndef _new_password(person: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Asked twice with the input hidden at a terminal; otherwise one line from stdin.\"\"\"\n    if _is_interactive():\n        try:\n            first = getpass.getpass(f\"Password for {person}: \")\n            second = getpass.getpass(\"Repeat the password: \")\n        except EOFError:\n            raise KeyboardInterrupt from None\n        if first != second:\n            raise Failure(\"The passwords don't match. Nothing was changed.\", exit_code=USAGE)\n        return first\n    line = sys.stdin.readline()\n    if not line:\n        raise Failure(\n            \"No password given, and there's no terminal to ask on.\",\n            hint=\"Run this in a terminal, or pipe the password on stdin.\",\n            exit_code=USAGE,\n        )\n    return line.rstrip(\"\\r\\n\")\n\n\ndef _error(message: str, hint: str | None = None) -> None:\n    typer.echo(f\"Error: {message}\", err=True)\n    if hint:\n        typer.echo(f\"  {hint}\", err=True)\n\n\ndef _next_steps(commands: list[str]) -> None:\n    for i, step in enumerate(commands):\n        typer.echo(f\"{'Next:' if i == 0 else '     '} {step}\", err=True)\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    app(prog_name=\"mc-admin\")\n"
}
```

**🔧 Write**

````json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/pritanbarai/Desktop/JOBS/mutinex_mission_control/.worktrees/m17-readme/README.md",
  "content": "# Mission Control\n\nA multi-tenant API and CLI for planning space missions. Organisations manage crew and their skills, Mission Leads build missions and get crew suggestions from a deterministic matcher, and Directors approve missions before they go active.\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- Python 3.12, with SQLite 3.37 or later (standard Python 3.12 builds have it)\n- [uv](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/). It can install Python too: `uv python install 3.12`\n\n## Quick start\n\n```bash\nuv sync\nuv run mc-admin seed --reset      # demo data in ./mission_control.db\nuv run mc-server                  # API on http://127.0.0.1:8000; leave it running\n```\n\nIn a second terminal, from the same folder:\n\n```bash\nuv run mc login --email lee@aurora.example    # password: orbital launch window\nuv run mc missions list\n```\n\nEvery demo account's password is `orbital launch window`. `mc login` asks for it with the input hidden.\n\n## Demo: the golden path\n\nLunar Gateway Resupply (mission 1) is a draft with three empty slots. Take it all the way to Completed. Each `mc login` replaces the previous session, so one terminal is enough. Start from a fresh seed (`uv run mc-admin seed --reset`, with the server stopped).\n\n**1. Lee, the owning Lead, asks the matcher for crew and proposes its suggestion.**\n\n```bash\nuv run mc login --email lee@aurora.example\nuv run mc inbox\nuv run mc missions match 1                     # Commander → Ana, Flight Engineer → Chen, Specialist → Dana\nuv run mc missions match 1 --apply             # proposes them as assignments 11, 12 and 13\n```\n\n**2. Each crew member accepts their proposal.**\n\n```bash\nuv run mc login --email ana@aurora.example\nuv run mc assignments list --pending\nuv run mc assignments accept 11\nuv run mc login --email chen@aurora.example\nuv run mc assignments accept 12\nuv run mc login --email dana@aurora.example\nuv run mc assignments accept 13\n```\n\n**3. Lee submits it. Aurora needs two approvals.**\n\n```bash\nuv run mc login --email lee@aurora.example\nuv run mc missions submit 1\n```\n\n**4. Directors approve. Ruth created the mission, so she can't approve it.**\n\n```bash\nuv run mc login --email ruth@aurora.example\nuv run mc missions approve 1                   # refused: Ruth created this mission\nuv run mc login --email marco@aurora.example\nuv run mc missions approve 1                   # pending approval, 1 of 2\nuv run mc login --email ingrid@aurora.example\nuv run mc missions approve 1                   # 2 of 2: the mission is active\n```\n\n**5. Lee completes it, and the history shows every step with who did it and when.**\n\n```bash\nuv run mc login --email lee@aurora.example\nuv run mc missions complete 1\nuv run mc missions history 1\n```\n\n**Tenant isolation.** Sam is a Director at Helix Orbital. Aurora's missions don't exist as far as he can tell.\n\n```bash\nuv run mc login --email sam@helix.example\nuv run mc missions list                        # only Helix's Payload Demo 1\nuv run mc missions show 1                      # refused: looks exactly like an unknown ID\n```\n\nEvery command has `--help`, and most take `--json`. `mc` exits with `0` OK, `1` refused, `2` usage, `3` not logged in, `4` server unreachable or busy, `130` interrupted.\n\n## Demo accounts\n\nAll passwords are `orbital launch window`. Mission and crew details are in [PRD §9](docs/prd.md#9-seed-data-and-scenarios).\n\n| Name | Email | Role | Organisation |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Ruth Adeyemi | ruth@aurora.example | Director | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Marco Bianchi | marco@aurora.example | Director | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Ingrid Holm | ingrid@aurora.example | Director | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Lee Park | lee@aurora.example | Mission Lead | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Nora Quinn | nora@aurora.example | Mission Lead | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Ana Okafor | ana@aurora.example | Mission Lead | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Ben Ito | ben@aurora.example | Crew Member | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Chen Wu | chen@aurora.example | Crew Member | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Dana Reyes | dana@aurora.example | Crew Member | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Eli Novak | eli@aurora.example | Crew Member | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Fay Moreau | fay@aurora.example | Crew Member | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Gus Patel | gus@aurora.example | Crew Member | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Hana Sato | hana@aurora.example | Crew Member | Aurora Space Agency |\n| Sam Rivera | sam@helix.example | Director | Helix Orbital |\n| Priya Nair | priya@helix.example | Mission Lead | Helix Orbital |\n| Kai Tanaka | kai@helix.example | Crew Member | Helix Orbital |\n| Lena Fischer | lena@helix.example | Crew Member | Helix Orbital |\n| Omar Haddad | omar@helix.example | Crew Member | Helix Orbital |\n| Tess Moreno | tess@helix.example | Crew Member | Helix Orbital |\n\nAurora needs 2 approvals per mission and Helix needs 1. Marco and Ana also have crew profiles, so they can be rostered.\n\n## Operators: `mc-admin`\n\n`mc-admin` works on the database file directly, so run it on the machine that runs `mc-server`. There's no self-service signup: organisations come from the seed or from `create-org`.\n\n```bash\nuv run mc-admin init-db                        # an empty database, if you don't want the demo data\nuv run mc-admin create-org --name \"Orion Labs\" --director-email zoe@orion.example --director-name \"Zoe Lin\"\n```\n\n`create-org` asks for the Director's password twice with the input hidden (at least 15 characters). Without a terminal, pipe it on stdin. The new organisation needs 1 approval per mission and starts with an empty skill catalogue. Its Director logs in with `mc` and sets up the rest (`mc skills add`, `mc users add`, `mc org set-approvals`).\n\n`mc-admin seed` refuses a database that already has data. `seed --reset` deletes the file and everything in it first. Stop the server before resetting.\n\n## Settings\n\nEnvironment variables, all optional:\n\n| Variable | Default | Used by |\n|---|---|---|\n| `MC_DATABASE_PATH` | `mission_control.db` in the current folder | `mc-server`, `mc-admin` |\n| `MC_TOKEN_TTL_HOURS` | `12` | `mc-server` |\n| `MC_DB_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS` | `5000` | `mc-server`, `mc-admin` |\n| `MC_API_URL` | `http://127.0.0.1:8000` | `mc` |\n\n`mc-server` listens on `127.0.0.1:8000`. `mc` keeps its session token in `~/.config/mission-control/credentials.json`, readable only by you.\n\n## Tests and lint\n\n```bash\nuv run pytest\nuv run ruff check\nuv run ruff format --check\n```\n\nEvery test gets its own temporary SQLite file. `tests/cli/test_readme_smoke.py` runs this README's `mc-admin`, `mc-server` and `mc` commands for real, in order.\n\n## Documents\n\n| Document | What's in it |\n|---|---|\n| [`docs/brief.md`](docs/brief.md) | The assessment brief |\n| [`docs/prd.md`](docs/prd.md) | Behaviour: roles, business rules, acceptance criteria, seed data and scenarios |\n| [`docs/technical-design.md`](docs/technical-design.md) | Structure: architecture, schema, API, CLI, delivery plan, guidelines for coding agents, trade-offs |\n| [`docs/phase-reviews.md`](docs/phase-reviews.md) | Where each phase diverged from the plan, and why |\n| [`docs/cli-ux.md`](docs/cli-ux.md) | Mock CLI sessions for the matcher |\n| [`docs/backlog.md`](docs/backlog.md) | Decisions deliberately left open for later |\n\n## Code layout\n\n```\nsrc/mission_control/\n  domain/       every business rule, as pure functions: dates, eligibility, matcher, lifecycle, permissions\n  infra/        SQLite connections, schema, tenant-scoped repositories, password hashing and tokens\n  services/     one use case per function: one transaction, its checks and its audit event\n  api/          FastAPI app (mc-server): validates input, resolves the caller, maps errors\n  cli/          the mc command, a client of the HTTP API\n  seed/         the demo data, shared by mc-admin and the tests\n  admin_cli.py  mc-admin: init-db, seed, create-org\ntests/          unit/ (domain), integration/ (services, database), api/, cli/\n```\n\n`domain/` imports nothing from the other layers, and the CLI holds no business rules: it only calls the API. The [design](docs/technical-design.md#2-architecture) explains why.\n\n## How it was built\n\nThe design documents were written first and then used to direct a coding agent (Claude Code):\n\n- [`CLAUDE.md`](CLAUDE.md) holds the agent's rules: one approved module at a time, tests first, a real run, a read-only review, and no commit without approval. Independent modules ran in parallel git worktrees.\n- [Design §9](docs/technical-design.md#9-delivery-plan) is the module-by-module plan, and [§10](docs/technical-design.md#10-guidelines-for-implementing-agents) the guidelines the agent followed.\n- [`docs/phase-reviews.md`](docs/phase-reviews.md) records each phase's divergences and its end-of-phase review.\n- The full, unedited AI transcripts are submitted with this repository.\n"
}
````

  "content": "\"\"\"M17 (design §9): smoke-test the README. Its `mc-admin`, `mc-server` and `mc` commands are read\nfrom the README itself and run for real, in order, as separate processes.\n\n- The commands are every `uv run mc…` line inside a ```bash block. A line whose comment starts\n  with \"refused\" must exit 1; every other line must exit 0.\n- `mc-admin` and `mc` run as the installed console scripts (what `uv run` starts), in a temporary\n  working folder with a temporary HOME, so the default `mission_control.db` and the saved session\n  never touch the developer's own.\n- `mc-server` is started in-process on a free port instead of 8000, and `mc` is pointed at it with\n  `MC_API_URL`.\n- Passwords are piped on stdin, which the README says works without a terminal.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport importlib.util\nimport os\nimport shlex\nimport sqlite3\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport threading\nimport time\nfrom collections.abc import Generator\nfrom contextlib import contextmanager\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom mission_control.infra import security\nfrom mission_control.seed.data import DEMO_PASSWORD\n\nREADME = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / \"README.md\"\nPROGRAMS = (\"mc\", \"mc-admin\", \"mc-server\")\nNEW_DIRECTOR_PASSWORD = \"a passphrase for the new director\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Step:\n    line: int  # in the README, for failure messages\n    args: tuple[str, ...]  # without `uv run`, e.g. (\"mc\", \"login\", \"--email\", \"lee@...\")\n    refused: bool\n\n    @property\n    def program(self) -> str:\n        return self.args[0]\n\n    def __str__(self) -> str:\n        return f\"README.md:{self.line}: {shlex.join(self.args)}\"\n\n\ndef readme_steps() -> list[Step]:\n    steps = []\n    in_bash = False\n    for number, text in enumerate(README.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines(), start=1):\n        if text.startswith(\"```\"):\n            in_bash = not in_bash and text.strip() == \"```bash\"\n            continue\n        if not in_bash:\n            continue\n        command, _, comment = text.partition(\" #\")\n        words = shlex.split(command)\n        if words[:2] == [\"uv\", \"run\"] and len(words) > 2 and words[2] in PROGRAMS:\n            refused = comment.strip().lower().startswith(\"refused\")\n            steps.append(Step(number, tuple(words[2:]), refused))\n    return steps\n\n\ndef _has(module: str) -> bool:\n    try:\n        return importlib.util.find_spec(module) is not None\n    except ModuleNotFoundError:\n        return False\n\n\nPHASE_4_READY = _has(\"mission_control.cli.main\") and _has(\"mission_control.api.routers\")\n\n\n# --- The command list  assert first == [\n        (\"mc-admin\", \"seed\", \"--reset\"),\n        (\"mc-server\",),\n        (\"mc\", \"login\", \"--email\", \"lee@aurora.example\"),\n    ]\n\n\ndef test_s1_readme_demo_follows_the_golden_path():\n    steps = [s.args for s in readme_steps()]\n    approvals = [\n        (steps[i - 1][-1], s[-1], s in steps)\n        for i, s in enumerate(steps)\n        if s[:3] == (\"mc\", \"missions\", \"approve\")\n    ]\n    assert [who for who, _, _ in approvals] == [\n        \"ruth@aurora.example\",\n        \"marco@aurora.example\",\n        \"ingrid@aurora.example\",\n    ]\n    for expected in [\n        (\"mc\", \"missions\", \"match\", \"1\", \"--apply\"),\n        (\"mc\", \"assignments\", \"accept\", \"11\"),\n        (\"mc\", \"assignments\", \"accept\", \"12\"),\n        (\"mc\", \"assignments\", \"accept\", \"13\"),\n        (\"mc\", \"missions\", \"submit\", \"1\"),\n        (\"mc\", \"missions\", \"complete\", \"1\"),\n        (\"mc\", \"missions\", \"history\", \"1\"),\n    ]:\n        assert expected in steps\n    assert steps.index((\"mc\", \"missions\", \"submit\", \"1\")) < steps.index(\n        (\"mc\", \"missions\", \"complete\", \"1\")\n    )\n\n\ndef test_ac8_ac27_only_ruths_approval_and_the_cross_tenant_read_are_expected_to_fail():\n    steps = readme_steps()\n    refused = [(steps[i - 1].args[-1], s.args) for i, s in enumerate(steps) if s.refused]\n    assert refused == [\n        (\"ruth@aurora.example\", (\"mc\", \"missions\", \"approve\", \"1\")),\n        (\"sam@helix.example\", (\"mc\", \"missions\", \"show\", \"1\")),\n    ]\n\n\n# --- Running it ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef workdir(tmp_path):\n    folder = tmp_path / \"work\"\n    (tmp_path / \"home\").mkdir()\n    folder.mkdir()\n    return folder\n\n\ndef _environment(workdir: Path, api_url: str | None) -> dict[str, str]:\n    env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith(\"MC_\")}\n    env.update(HOME=str(workdir.parent / \"home\"), NO_COLOR=\"1\")\n    if api_url:\n        env[\"MC_API_URL\"] = api_url\n    return env\n\n\ndef _script(name: str) -> str:\n    path = Path(sys.executable).parent / name\n    if not path.exists():\n        pytest.fail(f\"{name} isn't installed in this environment. Run `uv sync` first.\")\n    return str(path)\n\n\ndef _stdin(step: Step) -> str:\n    if step.args[:2] == (\"mc\", \"login\"):\n        return DEMO_PASSWORD + \"\\n\"\n    if step.args[:2] == (\"mc-admin\", \"create-org\"):\n        return NEW_DIRECTOR_PASSWORD + \"\\n\"\n    return \"\"\n\n\ndef _run(step: Step, workdir: Path, env: dict[str, str]) -> None:\n    result = subprocess.run(\n        [_script(step.program), *step.args[1:]],\n        cwd=workdir,\n        env=env,\n        input=_stdin(step),\n        capture_output=True,\n        text=True,\n        timeout=60,\n    )\n    expected = 1 if step.refused else 0\n    assert result.returncode == expected, (\n        f\"{step}\\nexpected exit {expected}, got {result.returncode}\\n\"\n        f\"--- stdout\\n{result.stdout}--- stderr\\n{result.stderr}\"\n    )\n    assert \"Traceback\" not in result.stdout + result.stderr, str(step)\n\n\n@contextmanager\ndef _server(database: Path) -> Generator[str]:\n    \"\"\"The API app on a free local port, in a background thread, for as long as the block runs.\"\"\"\n    import uvicorn\n\n    from mission_control.api.app import create_app\n    from mission_control.infra.settings import Settings\n\n    app = create_app(Settings(database_path=database))\n    server = uvicorn.Server(uvicorn.Config(app, host=\"127.0.0.1\", port=0, log_level=\"warning\"))\n    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.run, daemon=True)\n    thread.start()\n    deadline = time.monotonic() + 15\n    while not server.started:\n        if not thread.is_alive() or time.monotonic() > deadline:\n            pytest.fail(\"the API server didn't start\")\n        time.sleep(0.05)\n    port = server.servers[0].sockets[0].getsockname()[1]\n    try:\n        yield f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}\"\n    finally:\n        server.should_exit = True\n        thread.join(timeout=15)\n\n\ndef _rows(database: Path, sql: str) -> list[tuple]:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(database)\n    try:\n        return [tuple(r) for r in conn.execute(sql)]\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef test_r10_readme_mc_admin_commands_run(workdir):\n    \"\"\"Runs now: only `mc-admin` is needed.\"\"\"\n    env = _environment(workdir, api_url=None)\n    steps = [s for s in readme_steps() if s.program == \"mc-admin\"]\n    assert [s.args[1] for s in steps] == [\"seed\", \"init-db\", \"create-org\"]\n\n    for step in steps:\n        _run(step, workdir, env)\n\n    database = workdir / \"mission_control.db\"\n    assert _rows(database, \"SELECT name FROM organisations ORDER BY id\") == [\n        (\"Aurora Space Agency\",),\n        (\"Helix Orbital\",),\n        (\"Orion Labs\",),\n    ]\n    lee = _rows(database, \"SELECT password_hash FROM users WHERE email = 'lee@aurora.example'\")\n    assert security.verify_password(lee[0][0], DEMO_PASSWORD)\n    zoe = _rows(database, \"SELECT password_hash, role FROM users WHERE email = 'zoe@orion.example'\")\n    assert zoe[0][1] == \"director\"\n    assert security.verify_password(zoe[0][0], NEW_DIRECTOR_PASSWORD)\n\n\n@pytest.mark.skipif(\n    not PHASE_4_READY,\n    reason=\"mc (M16) and the API routers (M15) aren't on this branch yet; \"\n    \"this runs once Phase 4 is reconciled\",\n)\ndef test_ac1_readme_quick_start_and_demo_run_end_to_end(workdir):\n    database = workdir / \"mission_control.db\"\n    steps = readme_steps()\n    server_at = next(i for i, s in enumerate(steps) if s.program == \"mc-server\")\n\n    env = _environment(workdir, api_url=None)\n    for step in steps[:server_at]:\n        _run(step, workdir, env)\n\n    with _server(database) as url:\n        env = _environment(workdir, api_url=url)\n        for step in steps[server_at + 1 :]:\n            assert step.program != \"mc-server\", f\"{step}: the server is already running\"\n            _run(step, workdir, env)\n\n    assert _rows(database, \"SELECT status FROM missions WHERE id = 1\") == [(\"completed\",)]\n    assert _rows(\n        database, \"SELECT crew_profile_id, status FROM assignments WHERE mission_id = 1 ORDER BY id\"\n    ) == [(6, \"completed\"), (8, \"completed\"), (9, \"completed\")]\n    events = [e for (e,) in _rows(database, \"SELECT event FROM audit_events WHERE mission_id = 1\")]\n    assert events.count(\"mission.approved\") == 2  # Marco and Ingrid; Ruth's refusal wrote nothing\n    assert events[-1] == \"mission.completed\"\n"

*Rewrite step-order tests and rerun*

```bash
cd /Users/pritanbarai/Desktop/JOBS/mutinex_mission_control/.worktrees/m17-readme && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='tests/cli/test_readme_smoke.py'
lines=open(p).read().split('\n')
start=lines.index('def test_s1_readme_demo_follows_the_golden_path():')
end=lines.index('# --- Running it ---------------------------------------------------------------------------')
new='''def as_user(steps: list[Step]) -> list[tuple[str | None, Step]]:
    """Each step with the email of whoever the last `mc login` signed in."""
    who, result = None, []
    for step in steps:
        if step.args[:3] == ("mc", "login", "--email"):
            who = step.args[3]
        result.append((who, step))
    return result


def test_s1_readme_demo_follows_the_golden_path_in_order():
    s1_actions = {"match", "accept", "submit", "approve", "complete", "history"}
    done = [
        (who.split("@")[0], " ".join(step.args[1:]))
        for who, step in as_user(readme_steps())
        if step.program == "mc" and len(step.args) > 2 and step.args[2] in s1_actions
        and step.args[3] in ("1", "11", "12", "13")
    ]
    assert done == [
        ("lee", "missions match 1"),
        ("lee", "missions match 1 --apply"),
        ("ana", "assignments accept 11"),
        ("chen", "assignments accept 12"),
        ("dana", "assignments accept 13"),
        ("lee", "missions submit 1"),
        ("ruth", "missions approve 1"),
        ("marco", "missions approve 1"),
        ("ingrid", "missions approve 1"),
        ("lee", "missions complete 1"),
        ("lee", "missions history 1"),
    ]


def test_ac8_ac27_only_ruths_approval_and_the_cross_tenant_read_are_expected_to_fail():
    refused = [(who, step.args) for who, step in as_user(readme_steps()) if step.refused]
    assert refused == [
        ("ruth@aurora.example", ("mc", "missions", "approve", "1")),
        ("sam@helix.example", ("mc", "missions", "show", "1")),
    ]

'''
lines[start:end]=new.split('\n')
open(p,'w').write('\n'.join(lines))
EOF
uv run ruff format tests/cli && uv run pytest tests/cli -q -rs 2>&1 | tail -4
```

```
237	def _settings() -> Settings:
238	    try:
239	        return Settings.from_env(os.environ)
240	    except ValueError as e:
241	        raise Failure(f"{e}.", exit_code=USAGE) from None

243	
244	def _shown(path: Path) -> Path:
245	    """The database path as the operator should see it: absolute, so there's no doubt which
246	    file was used."""
247	    return path.expanduser().resolve()
248	
249	
250	@contextmanager
251	def _database(settings: Settings) -> Generator[sqlite3.Connection]:
252	    """A connection to the configured file, with database failures put into words."""
253	    path = _shown(settings.database_path)
254	    try:
255	        conn = connect(settings.database_path, busy_timeout_ms=settings.busy_timeout_ms)
256	    except sqlite3.OperationalError:
257	        raise Failure(
258	            f"Can't open the database at {path}.",
259	            hint="Check that its folder exists, or set MC_DATABASE_PATH to another file.",
260	        ) from None
261	    try:
262	        yield conn
263	    except DatabaseBusy:
264	        raise Failure(
265	            f"The database at {path} is busy. Nothing was changed.",
266	            hint="Try again in a moment.",
267	        ) from None
268	    except sqlite3.DatabaseError as e:
269	        if type(e) is not sqlite3.DatabaseError:  # constraint failures etc. are real bugs
270	            raise
271	        raise Failure(
272	            f"{path} isn't a Mission Control database. Nothing was changed.",
273	            hint="Set MC_DATABASE_PATH to another file, or replace it: mc-admin seed --reset",
274	        ) from None
275	    finally:
276	        conn.close()
277	
278	
279	def _prepare(conn: sqlite3.Connection, path: Path) -> bool:
280	    """Make sure the schema is there. True if it was created just now."""
281	    try:
282	        empty = conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0] == 0
283	        init_db(conn)
284	    except UnsupportedSQLite as e:
285	        raise Failure(f"{e}.", hint="Use a Python built with a newer SQLite.") from None
286	    except RuntimeError as e:  # a schema version this code doesn't know
287	        raise Failure(
288	            f"The database at {path} can't be used: {e}.",
289	            hint=f"Set MC_DATABASE_PATH to another file, or start over: {_RESET}",
290	        ) from None
291	    return empty
292	
293	
294	def _delete_database(path: Path) -> bool:
295	    """Remove the database file and SQLite's side files, so a stale log can't be replayed into
296	    the new one. True if there was a database to delete."""
297	    existed = path.exists()
298	    for victim in (path, *(path.with_name(path.name + s) for s in ("-wal", "-shm", "-journal"))):
299	        try:
300	            victim.unlink(missing_ok=True)
301	        except OSError as e:
302	            raise Failure(
303	                f"Can't delete {_shown(victim)}: {e.strerror}.",
304	                hint="Stop mc-server and check the file's permissions, then try again.",
```
